On a non-positive-definite\n// pivot, records the global diagonal index (+1) in *info and aborts the block.\n__global__ void potf2Kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n\n    if (tx == 0) {\n        failed = 0;\n    }\n    // Load block into shared memory (coalesced: tx indexes columns)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            A[(k + i) * n + k + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: A21 = A21 * L11^{-T}, where L11 is the nb x nb factored\n// diagonal block at (k, k) and A21 is the m x nb panel below it.\n// One thread per panel row.\n__global__ void trsmKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB];\n    for (int idx = threadIdx.x;                                                \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,               \\\n                   cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                     \\\n            exit(1);                                                              \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Panel/block size for the blocked factorization\nconstexpr int NB = 64;\n// Tile size for the trailing-matrix update kernel\nconstexpr int TILE = 32;\n\n// Factorize the nb x nb diagonal block starting at (k, k) in place.\n// Runs as a single thread block with NB threads. On a non-positive-definite\n// pivot, records the global diagonal index (+1) in *info and aborts the block.\n__global__ void potf2Kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n\n    if (tx == 0) {\n        failed = 0;\n    }\n    // Load block into shared memory (coalesced: tx indexes columns)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            s[i][tx] = A[(k + i) * n + k + tx];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tx == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                *info = (int)(k + j) + 1;\n                failed = 1;\n            } else {\n                s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (failed) {\n            return;\n        }\n        // Scale column j and apply the rank-1 update to the trailing block\n        if (tx > j && tx < nb) {\n            const double lij = s[tx][j] / s[j][j];\n            s[tx][j] = lij;\n            for (int t = j + 1; t <= tx; ++t) {\n                s[tx][t] -= lij * s[t][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored block back (lower triangle; upper is zeroed later)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            A[(k + i) * n + k + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: A21 = A21 * L11^{-T}, where L11 is the nb x nb factored\n// diagonal block at (k, k) and A21 is the m x nb panel below it.\n// One thread per panel row.\n__global__ void trsmKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB];\n    for (int idx = threadIdx.x; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        L[idx / nb][idx % nb] = A[(k + idx / nb) * n + k + idx % nb];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= m) {\n        return;\n    }\n    double* row = A + (k + nb + i) * n + k;\n\n    double r[NB];\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        r[t] = row[t];\n    }\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        double sum = r[j];\n        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n            sum -= r[t] * L[j][t];\n        }\n        r[j] = sum / L[j][j];\n    }\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        row[t] = r[t];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update (SYRK): A22 -= P * P^T, where P is the m x nb panel\n// at (k + nb, k) and A22 is the m x m trailing matrix at (k + nb, k + nb).\n// Only the lower triangle of A22 is updated. Shared-memory tiled.\n__global__ void syrkKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    const size_t bx = blockIdx.x;\n    const size_t by = blockIdx.y;\n    if (by < bx) {\n        return; // strictly above the diagonal: nothing to update\n    }\n    __shared__ double Ps[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Qs[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t off = k + nb;\n    const size_t i = by * TILE + ty; // row within trailing matrix\n    const size_t j = bx * TILE + tx; // column within trailing matrix\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Ps[ty][tx] = (by * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + by * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        Qs[ty][tx] = (bx * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + bx * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            acc += Ps[ty][t] * Qs[tx][t];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < m && j < m && i >= j) {\n        A[(off + i) * n + off + j] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the strictly upper triangular part of A\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < n && j < n && j > i) {\n        A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dInfo, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n\n        // Factorize diagonal block\n        potf2Kernel<<<1, NB>>>(dA, n, k, nb, dInfo);\n\n        int info = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        if (info != 0) {\n            // Matrix is not positive definite\n            printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n                   (size_t)(info - 1));\n            cudaFree(dA);\n            cudaFree(dInfo);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const size_t m = n - k - nb;\n        if (m > 0) {\n            // Panel solve below the diagonal block\n            const int trsmThreads = 128;\n            const unsigned trsmBlocks = (unsigned)((m + trsmThreads - 1) / trsmThreads);\n            trsmKernel<<<trsmBlocks, trsmThreads>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n\n            // Trailing-matrix update\n            const unsigned mb = (unsigned)((m + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n            dim3 grid(mb, mb);\n            dim3 block(TILE, TILE);\n            syrkKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(32, 8);\n        dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                  (unsigned)((n + block.y - 1) / block.y));\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; On a non-positive-definite\n// pivot, records the global diagonal index (+1) in *info and aborts the block.\n__global__ void potf2Kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n\n    if (tx == 0) {\n        failed = 0;\n    }\n    // Load block into shared memory (coalesced: tx indexes columns)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            s[i][tx] = A[(k + i) * n + k + tx];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tx == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                *info = (int)(k + j) + 1;\n                failed = 1;\n            } else {\n                s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (failed) {\n            return;\n        }\n        // Scale column j\n        if (tx > j && tx < nb) {\n            s[tx][j] /= s[j][j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        // Rank-1 update of the trailing block\n        if (tx > j && tx < nb) {\n            const double lij = s[tx][j];\n            for (int t = j + 1; t <= tx; ++t) {\n                s[tx][t] -= lij * s[t][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored block back (lower triangle; upper is zeroed later)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            A[(k + i) * n + k + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: A21 = A21 * L11^{-T}, where L11 is the nb x nb factored\n// diagonal block at (k, k) and A21 is the m x nb panel below it.\n// One thread per panel row.\n__global__ void trsmKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB];\n    for (int idx = threadIdx.x; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        L[idx / nb][idx % nb] = A[(k + idx / nb) * n + k + idx % nb];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= m) {\n        return;\n    }\n    double* row = A + (k + nb + i) * n + k;\n\n    double r[NB];\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        r[t] = row[t];\n    }\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        double sum = r[j];\n        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n            sum -= r[t] * L[j][t];\n        }\n        r[j] = sum / L[j][j];\n    }\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        row[t] = r[t];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update (SYRK): A22 -= P * P^T, where P is the m x nb panel\n// at (k + nb, k) and A22 is the m x m trailing matrix at (k + nb, k + nb).\n// Only the lower triangle of A22 is updated. Shared-memory tiled.\n__global__ void syrkKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    const size_t bx = blockIdx.x;\n    const size_t by = blockIdx.y;\n    if (by < bx) {\n        return; // strictly above the diagonal: nothing to update\n    }\n    __shared__ double Ps[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Qs[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t off = k + nb;\n    const size_t i = by * TILE + ty; // row within trailing matrix\n    const size_t j = bx * TILE + tx; // column within trailing matrix\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Ps[ty][tx] = (by * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + by * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        Qs[ty][tx] = (bx * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + bx * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            acc += Ps[ty][t] * Qs[tx][t];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < m && j < m && i >= j) {\n        A[(off + i) * n + off + j] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the strictly upper triangular part of A\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < n && j < n && j > i) {\n        A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dInfo, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n\n        // Factorize diagonal block\n        potf2Kernel<<<1, NB>>>(dA, n, k, nb, dInfo);\n\n        int info = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        if (info != 0) {\n            // Matrix is not positive definite\n            printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n                   (size_t)(info - 1));\n            cudaFree(dA);\n            cudaFree(dInfo);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const size_t m = n - k - nb;\n        if (m > 0) {\n            // Panel solve below the diagonal block\n            const int trsmThreads = 128;\n            const unsigned trsmBlocks = (unsigned)((m + trsmThreads - 1) / trsmThreads);\n            trsmKernel<<<trsmBlocks, trsmThreads>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n\n            // Trailing-matrix update\n            const unsigned mb = (unsigned)((m + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n            dim3 grid(mb, mb);\n            dim3 block(TILE, TILE);\n            syrkKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(32, 8);\n        dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                  (unsigned)((n + block.y - 1) / block.y));\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    \n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n    \n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n    \n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":325,"oldLines":6,"newStart":325,"newLines":10,"lines":["     printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);","     printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");","     ","+    // Initialize the CUDA context up front so it is not attributed","+    // to the timed decomposition","+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));","+","     // Allocate matrix","     std::vector<double> A(n * n);","     std::vector<double> A_orig;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

                                    \\\n            exit(1);                                                              \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Panel/block size for the blocked factorization\nconstexpr int NB = 64;\n// Tile size for the trailing-matrix update kernel\nconstexpr int TILE = 32;\n\n// Factorize the nb x nb diagonal block starting at (k, k) in place.\n// Runs as a single thread block with NB threads. On a non-positive-definite\n// pivot, records the global diagonal index (+1) in *info and aborts the block.\n__global__ void potf2Kernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, int* info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n\n    if (tx == 0) {\n        failed = 0;\n    }\n    // Load block into shared memory (coalesced: tx indexes columns)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            s[i][tx] = A[(k + i) * n + k + tx];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tx == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                *info = (int)(k + j) + 1;\n                failed = 1;\n            } else {\n                s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (failed) {\n            return;\n        }\n        // Scale column j\n        if (tx > j && tx < nb) {\n            s[tx][j] /= s[j][j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        // Rank-1 update of the trailing block\n        if (tx > j && tx < nb) {\n            const double lij = s[tx][j];\n            for (int t = j + 1; t <= tx; ++t) {\n                s[tx][t] -= lij * s[t][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factored block back (lower triangle; upper is zeroed later)\n    for (int i = 0; i < nb; ++i) {\n        if (tx < nb) {\n            A[(k + i) * n + k + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Panel solve: A21 = A21 * L11^{-T}, where L11 is the nb x nb factored\n// diagonal block at (k, k) and A21 is the m x nb panel below it.\n// One thread per panel row.\n__global__ void trsmKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    __shared__ double L[NB][NB];\n    for (int idx = threadIdx.x; idx < nb * nb; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        L[idx / nb][idx % nb] = A[(k + idx / nb) * n + k + idx % nb];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= m) {\n        return;\n    }\n    double* row = A + (k + nb + i) * n + k;\n\n    double r[NB];\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        r[t] = row[t];\n    }\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        double sum = r[j];\n        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n            sum -= r[t] * L[j][t];\n        }\n        r[j] = sum / L[j][j];\n    }\n    for (int t = 0; t < nb; ++t) {\n        row[t] = r[t];\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing-matrix update (SYRK): A22 -= P * P^T, where P is the m x nb panel\n// at (k + nb, k) and A22 is the m x m trailing matrix at (k + nb, k + nb).\n// Only the lower triangle of A22 is updated. Shared-memory tiled.\n__global__ void syrkKernel(double* A, size_t n, size_t k, int nb, size_t m) {\n    const size_t bx = blockIdx.x;\n    const size_t by = blockIdx.y;\n    if (by < bx) {\n        return; // strictly above the diagonal: nothing to update\n    }\n    __shared__ double Ps[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Qs[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t off = k + nb;\n    const size_t i = by * TILE + ty; // row within trailing matrix\n    const size_t j = bx * TILE + tx; // column within trailing matrix\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < nb; kk += TILE) {\n        Ps[ty][tx] = (by * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + by * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        Qs[ty][tx] = (bx * TILE + ty < m && kk + tx < nb)\n                         ? A[(off + bx * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            acc += Ps[ty][t] * Qs[tx][t];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < m && j < m && i >= j) {\n        A[(off + i) * n + off + j] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero out the strictly upper triangular part of A\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* A, size_t n) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < n && j < n && j > i) {\n        A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dA, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dInfo, 0, sizeof(int)));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n\n        // Factorize diagonal block\n        potf2Kernel<<<1, NB>>>(dA, n, k, nb, dInfo);\n\n        int info = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        if (info != 0) {\n            // Matrix is not positive definite\n            printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n                   (size_t)(info - 1));\n            cudaFree(dA);\n            cudaFree(dInfo);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const size_t m = n - k - nb;\n        if (m > 0) {\n            // Panel solve below the diagonal block\n            const int trsmThreads = 128;\n            const unsigned trsmBlocks = (unsigned)((m + trsmThreads - 1) / trsmThreads);\n            trsmKernel<<<trsmBlocks, trsmThreads>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n\n            // Trailing-matrix update\n            const unsigned mb = (unsigned)((m + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n            dim3 grid(mb, mb);\n            dim3 block(TILE, TILE);\n            syrkKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(32, 8);\n        dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                  (unsigned)((n + block.y - 1) / block.y));\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; A[(off + bx * TILE + ty) * n + k + kk + tx]\n                         : 0.0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            acc += Ps[ty][t] * Qs[tx][t];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < m && j < m && i >= j) {\n        A[(off + i) * n + off + j] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n// C = M * M^T (full matrix), shared-memory tiled. Used for generating the\n// positive definite input matrix and for validating the factorization.\n__global__ void multMMtKernel(const double* M, double* C, size_t n) {\n    __shared__ double Ps[TILE][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Qs[TILE][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.y * TILE + ty;\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * TILE + tx;\n\n    double acc = 0.0;\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < n; kk += TILE) {\n        Ps[ty][tx] = (i < n && kk + tx < n) ? k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n\n        // Factorize diagonal block\n        potf2Kernel<<<1, NB>>>(dA, n, k, nb, dInfo);\n\n        int info = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        if (info != 0) {\n            // Matrix is not positive definite\n            printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n                   (size_t)(info - 1));\n            cudaFree(dA);\n            cudaFree(dInfo);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const size_t m = n - k - nb;\n        if (m > 0) {\n            // Panel solve below the diagonal block\n            const int trsmThreads = 128;\n            const unsigned trsmBlocks = (unsigned)((m + trsmThreads - 1) / trsmThreads);\n            trsmKernel<<<trsmBlocks, trsmThreads>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n\n            // Trailing-matrix update\n            const unsigned mb = (unsigned)((m + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n            dim3 grid(mb, mb);\n            dim3 block(TILE, TILE);\n            syrkKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n, k, nb, m);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        dim3 block(32, 8);\n        dim3 grid((unsigned)((n + block.x - 1) / block.x),\n                  (unsigned)((n + block.y - 1) / block.y));\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, n);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU\n    gpuMultMMt(B, A, n);\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1;
